## 8. 助手 · 2026-10-12T03:33:18.601Z

**已确认两个 P0 阶段一集成缺陷：合法 refill 的 owner 身份丢失，以及 secondary merge 被错误地当成重新分配。** 它们使 MSHR0 永久停住，最后才触发 `[264000]` watchdog。

完整证据、源码对照和提取命令见 [RCA 报告](/data1/gzh/EC/AI/TMP/rca_phase1_264000_20261012/report.md)。本轮未修改源码、未生成硬件、未运行仿真；已核对原 working-tree diff 未改变。

# 1. Phase-1 Spec

阶段一必须实现 local ownership、clear/refill ordering、replay 实际完成后释放和 exactly-once，同时保留正常 miss、secondary merge、MMIO 与 coherence 功能。

revision 2 明确区分 **slot epoch** 与 **逐 image generation**。当前将 `lineGeneration` 定义为 allocation generation 的临时方案，不能视为完整阶段一验收通过。完整 preservation 链仍属于阶段二。

# 2. Relevant Working-Tree Changes

HEAD 为 `a68183c83`；staged 为空，阶段一 RTL 均未提交。

| 文件 | 相关变化 |
|---|---|
| `dcache.scala` | 新增 slot 身份分配、S3/refill gate；MSHR 请求重建遗漏新增字段 |
| `mshrs.scala` | refill 携带身份；RPQ/SDQ 改为 terminal completion 后释放 |
| `lsu.scala` | 新增 effect/owner 字段、replay retry；功能 STQ 完成仍未完全脱离 strict 统计 token |
| `NBDcache.scala` | 共享 refill Bundle 增加本地身份，Rocket 补零 |

本次没有新增 L2 preservation FSM。

# 3. Failure @ ~264000

直接错误是 [core.scala:1783](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/exu/core.scala:1783) 的 `Pipeline has hung`，阈值为连续 **262144 个周期无 ROB commit**。

问题事务：

- MSHR0/source0，STQ6 store：`0x80087ff0`
- 紧接 STQ7 store：`0x80087ff8`
- 同 line：`0x80087fc0`，L1 set255/way0
- refill 身份 `(epoch,generation)=(0,0)`，slot 身份却为 `(2,2)`

# 4. First Bad Event

以下是 FST 时间；`FST=2×trace_count+half_cycle`。

| 时间 | 证据 |
|---|---|
| 723 | 更早的 MMIO 接管错误推进 cache slot 身份；这是全 run 最早已定位的 owner 违例 |
| **2785** | 问题 line 首次 primary 分配：slot→`(1,1)`，MSHR 仍保存 `(0,0)` |
| **2787** | 合法 secondary store 合并，却再次使 slot→`(2,2)` |
| 2851 | 四个 GrantData beats 已完整接管 |
| **2859** | MSHR0进入 `s_commit_line`；refill valid=1、owner_match=0、ready=0 |
| 2877 | 最后一次 ROB commit 接收，watchdog 归零 |
| 3113以后 | MSHR0持续占 LineBuffer 读口，MSHR1 load响应也无法推进 |
| **527165** | watchdog 达到阈值，随后报错 |

精确关系：

```text
(527165 − 2877) / 2 = 262144 = 2^18
```

日志 `%t` 的舍入将约 263583 显示为 `[264000]`。

# 5. Root Cause

**P0-1：新增身份字段漏接。**

[dcache.scala:941](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/dcache.scala:941) 使用 `DontCare` 后逐字段构造 MSHR 请求，遗漏：

```text
slot_epoch、line_generation、effect_token_valid、effect_token
```

生成 RTL 将 MSHR 分配身份写成常量0，当前波形与之吻合。它破坏了“被接收的事务 descriptor 与 destination slot 属于同一 live owner”的 invariant。

**P0-2：新 owner 分类错误。**

[dcache.scala:865](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/dcache.scala:865) 用 `!secondary_miss` 推断新分配。但 [mshrs.scala:842](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/mshrs.scala:842) 的旧信号实际表示：

```text
同 set/way、不同 tag 的冲突
```

合法同址 secondary 的该信号为0，于是再次推进 slot 身份。通用 `req.fire` 还包括 MMIO，导致更早的错误 slot 更新。

两项都可造成 refill 永久失配，必须一起修。

# 6. Regression Proof

```text
HEAD：
Grant → refill直接写入 → replay → metadata publish

Working tree：
slot递增 + MSHR身份漏接/secondary再次递增
→ 新增refill gate拒绝
→ refill_ctr永久为0
→ load/ROB停顿
→ watchdog
```

手工请求构造虽是旧代码，**新增 Bundle 契约与消费 gate 是阶段一引入的**。固定优先级 LineBuffer 则放大了新增永久停顿。

# 7. Impact on Existing Functions

已观察到 store miss/refill/install、secondary merge、load 响应、共享 LineBuffer 进度和 GrantAck 延迟受到影响。

本 run 全程没有 replay、shadow clear 或 L1 data-write fire，无法据此验证旧 clear race、replay 混写或 SC/AMO 已修好。

# 8. Minimal Fix Direction

**Fix A：成组修复本地准入和身份传递，保留 ownership gate。**

1. 根据实际接受的 **cacheable primary allocation** 分配 owner；secondary继承主 MSHR 的 way/owner，MMIO不更新 cache slot。
2. 构造当前请求的 effective/admitted payload，完整传递身份与 effect token。
3. 真实 mismatch 必须有明确终止/重查流程，不能永久拉低 ready 并占用 LineBuffer。

还必须注意：[S2 的身份赋值](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/dcache.scala:870) 写入的是 `RegNext` 的**下一拍**。仅补 `MSHR.bits := s2_req` 仍可能错拍。

关闭此次 hang 后，阶段一还需完成逐 image generation、统计无关的 STQ 功能完成、较老 store 排序和完整 clear drain，才能满足 revision 2。

# 9. Missing Evidence

**确认本次 RCA 不需要新运行证据。**

修复后最小复验集合：primary/secondary/MMIO acceptance、destination owner、MSHR/RPQ 捕获身份、refill首末 fire、effect token、STQ/RPQ/SDQ terminal释放，以及 ROB恢复提交。随后再验证原 clear/refill 和 replay 混写案例。

# 10. AI/Debug Utilization

恢复了前序对话，阅读 revision 2、实施记录及 Debug 工具/历史 RCA；使用已有 FST reader 提取窄证据，未重新编译工具。

命中的模式是 **Bundle字段遗漏、Reg字段错拍、准入分类错误和共享资源放大停顿**。证据身份与原始提取保存在 [manifest](/data1/gzh/EC/AI/TMP/rca_phase1_264000_20261012/manifest.json)。

## Confirmed RCA — accept as facts

**P0-1**: DCache hand-builds MSHR payload with `:= DontCare`, then legacy assignments, but omits `slot_epoch`, `line_generation`, `effect_token_valid`, `effect_token`.
- `dcache.scala:941–961`.
- `BoomMSHR` captures `req := io.req` at `mshrs.scala:204`.
- Refills propagate req identity at356–357.
- Generated `BoomMSHR.sv:647–648` writes captured epoch/generation constant0.

**P0-2**: `dcache.scala:865` uses `s2_realloc && !mshrs.io.secondary_miss(0)` to infer owner allocation;963–968 increments owner on general req.fire.
- Actual legacy meaning at `mshrs.scala:842`:
  `idx_match && way_match && !tag_match`.
- A legal same-line merge has tag_match=true, secondary_miss=false, so it wrongly reallocates.
- General req.fire also accepts MMIO, so MMIO wrongly increments cache slot identity.
- Do not “fix” by changing legacy secondary_miss semantics: retain conflict use and introduce explicit acceptance classification.

**RegNext integration defect**:
- `s2_req = RegNext(s1_req)` at763.
-870–872 assigns identity to that Reg, changing **next-state**, not current S2 payload.
- Simply wiring MSHR bits from s2_req is insufficient.
- Generated sequential assignments confirm this.

- ROB head40 waits LDQ0/1 on first line, executed1/succeeded0.
- STQ6/7 already released at2787/2789 on early accepted responses, no actual effect. This is a closure defect.

  `(527165−2877)/2=262144`.

  enq accepted primary/secondary142–143; deq retained with inflight145.

- primary `req:=io.req`204; primary can be existing-tag permission upgrade; do not bump slot epoch for upgrade.
- load direct LineBuffer response265–305; RPQ pop on actual io.resp/LineBuffer ready, separate from replay.
- commit_line346–363 always `lb_read.valid=true`, refill.valid=lb_read.fire, ctr advances only refill.fire.
- replay364–383:
  deq bits, overridden primary req.way and line addr, mshr id; issue marks inflight; done/cancel clears inflight; deq on terminal; empty transition lacks explicit inflight/grant check.
- E394–399 currently waits after install/replay; revision2 requires E once full Grant securely captured, not gated by local replay/watermark. Treat whether Phase1 MUST carefully; source design §8 explicitly says this.

- Queue can branch-kill head while replay inflight; need inspect BranchKillableQueue implementation and ensure issued head identity remains owned until matching terminal, kill is acknowledged pre-effect only.

### Shared Rocket Bundle
`NBDcache.scala:25–31 L1DataWriteReq` adds slot_epoch/line_generation.
Rocket write inputs900–901,952–953 explicitly0; do not spread BOOM-specific image identities through Rocket/TL unnecessarily; consider BOOM-local extension/adapter for further fields.

## Important plan distinctions to resolve

No final implementation decisions yet this turn. Must make explicit, sound choices:

1. Separate **resident slot owner**, **MSHR allocation/transaction lifetime**, **per-image generation**, **per-request effect token**.
   - Primary miss installing a new line may reserve destination with new epoch; permission upgrade same line creates MSHR transaction but preserves slot epoch/image.
   - Replacement reservation must retain old victim snapshot/identity until old effects drained; do not relabel old bytes as incoming image.
   - Secondary inherits primary **way and owner context**, but distinct store has its **own** effect token, never copies primary store token.
   - Replay continuation preserves token/transaction/owner; pre-effect LSU retry preserves functional token but may relookup a new legal owner after proper transfer/cancel. Applied operation cannot be retried as new effect.

2. Admission wiring must avoid combinational loop:
   classification based raw address/cmd/cacheability/registered MSHR state and legacy resource ready; owner payload must not feed its own classification/ready.
   Define acceptance separately from offer; identities valid in same cycle before fire; register both descriptor and slot at same edge.

3. Refilling under a pinned MSHR should not legitimately lose owner.
   Immediate P0 repair likely simplest **impossible mismatch ⇒ explicit fatal invariant/protocol assertion, suppress writes and stop requesting shared LineBuffer**. Do not silently drop or abort to already-released STQ. User explicitly permits choosing impossible assertion; adding one identifies genuine invariant violation, not relaxing old assertion.
   If plan chooses recoverable abort, must first retain functional requester, handle partially installed rows, consumed D/E, RPQ/SDQ ownership and never duplicate AMO/MMIO. Avoid unnecessary abort FSM scope creep.

4. Phase1 MUST per-effect image generation; cannot defer local semantics to Phase2. Phase2 owns wire propagation/imported full provenance/certificates. Without D ImageKey, mark imported identity unknown/provisional, not fake BASE or fabricated trusted remote image. Serializing same-line modifications is allowed minimal implementation; do not increment generation at allocation/retry/failedSC/validation.

5. Early MSHR accepted response vs actual functional terminal:
   add traffic-independent STQ allocation/effect token and held completion. Keep strict stats separate (may observe mapped functional event).
   RPQ/SDQ only free matching terminal; RETRY clears inflight retaining head, CANCEL requires safe pre-effect architectural cancel or acknowledged transfer back to live LSU. Completion must include allocation/token to reject reused MSHR/STQ slots.
   Latest resident token alone is insufficient arbitrary duplicate history; use outstanding request lifecycle and terminal acknowledgement, not giant per-line history.

6. Clear/probe/install:
   freeze new conflicting grants, drain already-issued effects, recheck tag/epoch, snapshot WB, complete real TtoN invalidation, bump epoch only at owner transition, clear pending after live refs drained; S1 lookup binds observed epoch before clear so cannot adopt new epoch.
   WaitSafe must permit necessary probes/drain progress.
   Same-line grant hold for S3 backpressure and atomic data/meta publish.

7. Remaining Phase1 MUST also functional watermark/reset separation, package tail identity (`core.scala` active seq mux historically zero during snapshot), older store sequencing, write-domain not inferred from mutable primary cmd, full refill publish and no-data Grant handling, source/effect isolation.

## Next steps

1. Verify current sources/diff hashes equal RCA manifest (read-only).
2. Read targeted code ranges untruncated:
   dcache constructors540–785, req/admission850–1015, clear1120–1230, S2 responses/S31560–1807.
   mshrs1–180,190–430,530–650,680–860.

   BranchKillableQueue kill/flush/head semantics; other BoomDCacheReq constructors using `rg`.
   core package attribution and functional watermark/stat resets.
3. Build propagation table including new proposed allocation/effect completion fields and identify inactive control constructors: explicit valid/context absent rather than blindly setting a valid owner0.
4. Decide concrete minimal A plan with cycle diagram/table and mismatch fatal semantics versus recovery; explicitly state test/phase limits.
5. Develop B closure priorities and R1–R6 prevention/validation.
6. Deliver nine-section Chinese plan, ~implementation-ready detail without patch. Optionally save a dedicated planning document under AI/Design only if helpful; user asks “只出方案,” no need to write files. No source or unified-design edits.
7. Confirm untouched source/diff before final if tools used.

## 17. 助手 · 2026-10-12T04:10:23.572Z

# 1. Current Diagnosis

**应先成组修复 admission、当前拍 payload 和 refill owner 检查，再完成阶段一功能闭环。补字段与改 secondary 判断不能分别作为完整修复。**

已核对 [RCA](/data1/gzh/EC/AI/TMP/rca_phase1_264000_20261012/report.md)、[manifest](/data1/gzh/EC/AI/TMP/rca_phase1_264000_20261012/manifest.json)、[unified design revision 2](/data1/gzh/EC/AI/Design/cache_version_preservation_unified_design_20261011.md) 和当前源码。四个 RTL 文件及完整 working-tree diff 的 hash 与 RCA 一致。

- **P0-1：** DCache 重建 MSHR request 时遗漏 `slot_epoch / line_generation / effect_token_valid / effect_token`，事务捕获身份与 destination owner 不一致。
- **P0-2：** 把 `!secondary_miss` 当作新 allocation。旧信号实际表示同 set/way、不同 tag 的冲突，导致合法 secondary、MMIO 错误推进 cache owner。
- **必须共同修复的错拍：** 对 `s2_req=RegNext(s1_req)` 的字段赋值改变下一拍寄存器输入，不能生成当前 S2 接收事务的身份。

已证实的因果链为：

`错误 allocation + 身份漏接 → refill mismatch → 永久 backpressure → LineBuffer 被占用 → load/ROB 停顿 → watchdog`

旧实施说明将 allocation generation 作为阶段一 generation，并继续依赖 strict token 控制部分 STQ 生命周期；这两点与 revision 2 的阶段一验收要求不一致。

**本轮仅完成只读审计和方案；未修改任何文件，未编译、仿真或生成硬件。**

# 2. Ownership Contract

必须分开四类身份：

| 身份 | 表示什么 | 变化事件 |
|---|---|---|
| `SlotOwnerKey` | 本地 set/way 的占用生命周期 | 真正 invalidate、换 tag、重新分配 |
| `MSHR transaction token` | 某次 MSHR allocation，防止编号复用 | 接收新主事务 |
| `ImageKey / generation` | 准确的数据 image | 实际语义修改；纯搬移继承 |
| `request/effect token` | 某个功能请求及其一次副作用 | 请求生命周期开始；retry 不重新分配 |

## 准入分类

分类由 `BoomMSHRFile` 的实际选择点统一产生，覆盖全部 lane 和 prefetch；保留旧 `secondary_miss` 的 conflict 语义。

| 分类 | 准确条件与 owner 行为 |
|---|---|
| **Cacheable primary new-line** | 被选请求确实接收、cacheable、无可合并 MSHR、非同 tag resident；取得 destination reservation，创建 incoming owner |
| **Primary upgrade/state transition** | 新 MSHR 接收同 tag resident 请求，仅需权限或 coherence 状态推进；创建 MSHR 事务，继承 slot owner 和 image |
| **Legal secondary** | 命中已有 MSHR 的 line，原权限、队列和 write-domain 准入均满足；继承其 way、epoch、事务上下文及 refill image 依赖 |
| **MMIO** | 原地址管理器判定不可缓存，由 IOMSHR 接收；无 cache owner，保留请求/effect token |
| **Replay** | 已有 RPQ 请求继续执行；保留请求 token、MSHR allocation token、slot owner |
| **LSU retry** | 未产生 effect 的同一功能请求重试；token 不变。只有旧授权释放或责任转移被确认后，才能重新 lookup |
| **Conflict/replacement wait** | 当前 set/way 或 MSHR 冲突；沿旧 ready/nack 路径等待，不推进 owner。解除冲突后，接收的新 line 才成为 primary allocation |
| **Hit / other** | hit 继承 lookup owner；WB、probe、meta-read 使用各自 descriptor。它们不能因共享 `req.fire` 创建 incoming owner |

对所选 miss 请求：

```text
req.fire = primary_accept || secondary_accept || mmio_accept
allocation.fire = primary_accept && new_line && destination_reservation_granted
```

**secondary 继承 owner，不复制另一条 store 的 effect token。** 两条独立 store 各有自己的 token；同一请求的 replay/retry 才保留同一 token。

## Allocation、refill 与退休

- `allocation.fire` 捕获 destination、预定 epoch 和事务身份。
- 空闲且已 drain 的 slot 可在同一接受沿激活新 epoch。
- **有效 victim 采用 reservation：** primary 接收时保存 `reserved_epoch`，保留旧 resident key；完成旧引用 drain、snapshot 和必要 eviction 后，才激活 incoming epoch。旧 bytes 不能提前被标成 incoming image。
- epoch 更新由单一 owner-transition 点提交；secondary、MMIO、permission upgrade、普通 write 不递增 epoch。
- allocation 不产生语义 image generation。实际 store、成功 SC、AMO、replay write 才产生新 generation；失败 SC、load、retry、validation 不产生。
- refill 只在 destination install owner 已激活后检查 `{line,set,way,epoch,transaction}`。等待 victim drain 是正常等待，与真实 mismatch 分开。
- **真实 mismatch 选择 impossible assertion：** 报协议违例，禁止 array write，并停止该 MSHR 请求共享 LineBuffer；不 drop、abort 或自动 retry，不释放 RPQ/SDQ/STQ。
- MSHR 在 install、replay、响应、最后 write grant、E 等责任全部完成后退休；resident owner 则继续存活到真正 invalidate/reallocation。

## 无错拍的数据组织

建立独立的组合 `s2_effective_req`：

`当前 s2_raw_req + admission class + selected owner + request/effect token`

| 拍 | 数据与动作 |
|---|---|
| S1 | lookup 同时绑定所观察的 tag、way、epoch；clear 后不能重新借用新 epoch |
| S2，周期 C | 基于当前 raw 地址、命令和已寄存 MSHR 状态分类；组合形成 effective payload |
| C 的接受沿 | MSHR descriptor、RPQ、SDQ owner 记录及 destination reservation 捕获同一个 payload |
| C+1 | S3 捕获 C 拍获准的 hit/replay write grant；`s2_raw_req` 已属于下一请求 |

分类和基础 ready **不得读取自己正在补写的 owner 字段**。旧 conflict 判断使用 raw lookup way；secondary 的最终 way 从已匹配 MSHR 读取，避免组合反馈。

# 3. Required Code Changes

| 文件 / location | 当前行为 | 必须行为与恢复的 invariant |

| 同文件，1004–1011；MSHR `s_commit_line`【A】 | mismatch 只压低 ready，继续读 LineBuffer | install 前验证 owner；真实 mismatch 断言、停共享读口、禁写，保留请求责任 |

| MSHR refill、metadata publish、394–399【B】 | E 等 install/replay；退休缺少完整 inflight/grant 条件 | 全 Grant 捕获后 held E；install/provisional/replay/final publish 分开 |
| DCache 1532；LSU 2024；[core.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/exu/core.scala:2363)【B】 | RESET 清功能水位/pending；snapshot 尾部 seq 可变零 | 功能状态不受统计 RESET 影响；package 身份贯穿 producer 尾部 |
| [NBDcache.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/rocket-chip/src/main/scala/rocket/NBDcache.scala:25)【A/B】 | 共享 write Bundle 增加 BOOM 侧带，Rocket 补零 | 审计所有生产者；进一步 BOOM 专用 owner/txn 字段放本地扩展，避免侵入 Rocket/TL 协议 |

# 4. Bundle / Identity Propagation Audit

以下是最终阶段一契约；新增字段名称可调整，语义不能合并。

| Field | Producer | Capture point | Consumers | 必须保留经过 | Clear / retire point |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `owner_context_valid`、class、set/way | 统一 admission；控制 descriptor | effective request、MSHR | install、replay、S3、clear/probe | 所有 mux、队列和 rebuild | 对应 owner/事务退休 |
| `slot_epoch` | destination reservation / owner transition；hit 来自 lookup | S1 lookup、S2 effective、MSHR、RPQ、S3 grant | stale-write、refill、snapshot 检查 | secondary、replay、授权后的 pipeline | drain 后 owner transition |
| `line_generation` / image dependency | 准确当前/provisional image；实际 effect 分配新 serial | snapshot、S2 write grant、S3、install commit | image 一致性、write dependency | 搬移、grant 持有、重试上下文 | image 被新 image 替换；旧 descriptor 保留至完成 |
| `effect_token_valid/token` | 所有 store/SC/AMO 的功能生命周期；Hella client 独立入口 | STQ/client、request、RPQ、inflight、S3 | apply、duplicate completion、STQ done | nack、killed-S1、merge、replay/retry、MMIO | terminal 被确认且无旧引用 |
| `request/STQ allocation token` | STQ/LDQ 分配；store 可复用功能 token | request、RPQ、response/nack/completion | 拒绝队列 slot 复用后的迟到消息 | 全请求生命周期 | 匹配完成或合法取消 |
| `MSHR allocation token` | primary 接收点 | 主 descriptor、RPQ、replay、terminal | 防止 MSHR id 复用误完成 | 从 allocation 到最终退休 | 全事务责任耗尽 |
| `sdq_id + SDQ owner key` | SDQ allocation | RPQ、SDQ companion metadata、inflight | replay data、terminal free | issue、backpressure、retry | 匹配 terminal ACK |
| `replay_mshr_id`、`replay_retry` | replay 路由、pre-effect retry | S0–S3、nack、completion | 路由和重试处理 | 全 continuation | 对应 attempt 完成；编号不能单独授权 |
| `packet_seq / write_domain` | producer/LSQ；首个合法 write 建 domain | STQ、request、RPQ、MSHR domain、snapshot | 排序、local image attribution | snapshot 尾部、secondary、replay | 请求完成；resident seq 只随真实 image 更新 |
| terminal tag/status | effect/response/no-write/合法取消点 | held completion | LSU、RPQ、SDQ | completion backpressure | 匹配接收一次 |
| strict/statistics fields | 统计资格源 | 原统计 capture 点 | 计数器 | 统计窗口生命周期 | 仅统计消费/RESET |

审计规则：

- 覆盖 LSU、Hella incoming/wakeup、killed-S1、replay、prefetch、WB、probe、MSHR meta-read，以及所有 `Wire/Reg/Mux/DontCare/copy/queue`。
- SDQ 当前只保存 data，必须增加 companion owner 记录；不能靠 `sdq_id` 单独证明 ownership。
- 无 owner 的入口使用明确无效 context；“字段补零”不能代表合法 owner。
- secondary 保存主事务 refill 上下文；**每次 replay write 授权时**，在同一 owner 下取得准确当前 image dependency。不能永久使用 primary allocation 时的旧 generation。
- widths 按配置推导并检查范围；epoch、serial、token 接近回绕时停止新分配并报错，不能静默复用。

# 5. P0 Repair Plan

**A. Immediate P0 Repair — 最小成组修复**

1. **统一准入分类。** 在 MSHRFile 导出 primary-new-line、primary-upgrade、secondary、MMIO 和 selected owner；保留旧 conflict 信号。
2. **修当前拍 payload。** DCache 新建 `s2_effective_req`，删除以修改 `s2_req` 寄存器字段表达当前授权的做法；MSHR/RPQ 与 reservation 同沿捕获。
3. **完整传播。** 修 DCache→MSHR、secondary owner 覆盖、replay/S3 capture、prefetch 和控制入口构造；独立 store 保留独立 token。
4. **隔离 owner 更新。** 仅 new-line allocation 创建 reservation；MMIO、secondary、upgrade 不推进 epoch。replacement 保留 victim 身份，install 前激活 incoming owner。
5. **关闭永久占用。** 增加 install-owner 检查与 mismatch fault：在请求 LineBuffer 前检测，断言失败、禁写、撤共享读请求，禁止无完成依据的 pop/free。

A 可暂时保留当前 allocation-generation 侧带用于兼容，但必须明确它只是 owner 侧带，不能作为 ImageKey 或证书 subject。**B 完成准确 image generation 前，阶段一不能验收。**

A 的通过条件是当前 regression 的 primary/secondary/MMIO 身份正确、refill 实际推进、共享 load 和 ROB 恢复进度；它不证明旧 clear/replay 反例已修复。

# 6. Phase-1 Closure Plan

**B. Phase-1 Closure**

| 事项 | 范围 | 收敛要求 |
|---|---|---|
| 准确本地 image generation | **Phase-1 MUST** | slot epoch 与 image serial 分开；每次实际 store/成功 SC/AMO/replay write 产生新 generation，同值写也递增。初版串行同 line 修改，避免复杂 forwarding |
| 统计无关的 STQ completion | **Phase-1 MUST** | STQ enqueue 即分配功能身份，覆盖统计关闭及 pre-ROB-completion 的 SC/AMO；accepted 只表示接管。functional done、nack 匹配、执行指针推进和释放均不读取 strict token |
| MMIO terminal | **Phase-1 MUST** | 功能完成以匹配 D 响应及必要结果接收为准；A.fire 只是 transport acceptance。IOMSHR 保留 token 到完成，不因撤除 cache owner 更新而改变路由 |
| Older store ordering | **Phase-1 MUST** | 较老 store miss/upgrade 被接管后，年轻 store 暂停 issue，直到较老功能 token 完成；load 保留原内存顺序机制。clear、snapshot、install、owner retirement 等待已有老 effect/grant 耗尽 |
| 单包 write domain | **Phase-1 MUST** | 独立 `write_domain_valid/seq`；load primary 不建立 domain。首个 write 建立，后续不同包 write 拒绝 merge；不能用 mutable 主命令或 `max(request_seq)` 推导 |
| Clear / probe / replacement drain | **Phase-1 MUST** | `freeze → drain → tag/epoch recheck → snapshot/真实 TtoN → invalidate commit → retire`；涵盖 S1/S2/S3、补偿请求、MSHR、install 和响应。pending 只在匹配责任完成后清除 |
| 持有 S3 write grant | **Phase-1 MUST** | grant 保存 token、owner、image dependency、AMO 数据/旧值，直到真实 fire 或合法 pre-effect cancel；数据和 identity 同沿更新。旁路也必须对应合法 effect/grant |
| Replay exactly-once | **Phase-1 MUST** | 每 MSHR 锁存完整 inflight descriptor；completion 带 MSHR allocation、request/effect token、owner 和结果状态。APPLIED/LOAD_RESP/NO_WRITE_SC/合法 CANCEL 才 terminal；RETRY 仅清 inflight、保留 head/SDQ |
| RPQ kill 与 SDQ free | **Phase-1 MUST** | issued head 被 hold；branch/flush 记录取消责任，不能自动换 head 后用新 head 身份完成旧请求。free 来源为匹配 inflight terminal，支持同拍多个 completion |
| Full-line install / publish | **Phase-1 MUST** | 全 Grant 先进入 LineBuffer；最后真实 data fire 才提交 provisional identity。RPQ、inflight、S3 grant 全耗尽后 publish；no-data Grant 不写 refill、不重建 image |
| E 与 coherence 进度 | **Phase-1 MUST** | 完整 Grant 可靠捕获后发送 held E，独立于本地 watermark/replay；MSHR/有界暂存承担期间 probe。E 不表示 line 提前可见 |
| 功能状态与 package 尾部身份 | **Phase-1 MUST** | START/STOP/RESET 不清 owner、token、generation、pending、functional bitmap/safe；snapshot 尾部仍属于原 package，不能按 debug 状态输出零 seq |
| 观测与公平性 | **Phase-1 SHOULD** | acceptance/terminal 事件别名、停顿原因；在合法资源可用条件下验证共享读口有限占用和公平性 |
| 完整 preservation | **Phase-2** | C/D 完整 ImageKey/parent、`lower_preserved/outer_preserved`、certificate、L1 TtoB handoff、L2 predecessor/victim FSM、晚 preservation ACK |

补充两条边界：

- resident 的“最近一次 token”不足以证明任意重复请求。阶段一通过**唯一请求 owner、禁止重复在途、terminal 持有及确认**保证 exactly-once；已 applied 请求不能重新变成可执行 retry。
- Phase1 缺少 D provenance 时，外来 image 明确为 UNKNOWN/provisional；不从 seq0、allocation serial 或普通 Grant 伪造 BASE/证书。统一接口保留 `NEED_PRESERVATION`；严格证书许可随 Phase2 完成端接通。

# 7. Regression Safety

| 原路径 | 保护方式 |
|---|---|
| Normal hit、primary miss、secondary | 保留原权限和资源判定；新增分类描述实际接受。hit 绑定 lookup epoch；secondary 取主 MSHR way/owner |
| Conflict、replacement | 保留旧冲突 ready/nack；victim 与 incoming reservation 分开，禁止 relabel 旧 bytes |
| Refill、acquire、Grant/E | 保留 A/D/source/权限语义；仅 owner 授权 install；E 按 revision 2 独立完成 |
| Probe、ReleaseData | 老 effect drain 后取得完整 snapshot；发送 bytes 与身份来自固定 descriptor。WaitSafe 不长期占 WB 或堵必要 probe |
| MMIO | 沿原 IOMSHR A/D 状态机完成；不写 cache ownership metadata |
| Replay、retry | coherence forced-hit 可保留，但不能替代 owner/order/write admission；retry 保留功能 token |
| SC/AMO | 失败 SC 无 write/new generation；成功 SC/AMO 的实际写与结果接收共同满足 terminal，反压时保留计算结果 |

风险处理：

| 风险 | 预防方式 | 验证方式 |
|---|---|---|
| **R1 RegNext 错拍** | raw Reg 与 effective Wire 分开，同沿捕获 | 连续不同地址 primary/secondary/hit；逐拍比较 admitted、MSHR、S3 身份 |
| **R2 conflict/replacement 误分类** | 保留旧 conflict；显式 new-line/upgrade/reservation | 同 line merge、同 set 不同 tag、victim WB、permission upgrade |
| **R3 MMIO completion 受影响** | 仅隔离 cache owner；functional terminal 从原 D 路径产生 | 延迟 A/D/response；MMIO 与 refill 并发；owner 不变且完成一次 |
| **R4 abort/retry 重复 effect** | P0 mismatch 采用断言；普通 retry 必须 pre-effect，责任转移需确认 | owner fault 注入应立即报错且无写/free；effect 前后分别注入 nack/kill |
| **R5 token/completion 耦合** | 功能 token 独立、命名空间明确；统计仅观察完成 | 统计开/关、在途 RESET、STQ slot 复用、迟到 completion |
| **R6 allocation 冒充 image generation** | 独立计数与有效性；实际 effect 更新 image serial | 同包两次写 generation 不同；load/失败 SC/upgrade/validation 不变 |

# 8. Verification Checklist

**仿真由你运行；下面按修复依赖排序。**

| Level | 检查与通过条件 |
|---|---|
| **1：静态与构建检查** | 全 Bundle 生产者/消费者闭环；accept 分类唯一；无有效字段 `DontCare`；无 RegNext 当前拍授权；SDQ/free 身份匹配；功能状态不受 traffic RESET。后续依次 Scala、目标 elaboration、初始化/宽度、FIRRTL 组合环、generated RTL 检查；各级结果分别记录 |
| **2：本次定向 regression** | 先看 MMIO `0x02000004` 不改 cache owner；再看 line `0x80087fc0` primary 与 secondary：owner 创建一次、secondary 继承、descriptor/RPQ 身份一致、refill first/last fire、MSHR 退出 commit_line、另一 load 获得 LineBuffer、ROB 恢复 commit |
| **3：阶段一功能复验** | hit → primary → same-line secondary → conflict/replacement → upgrade/no-data Grant → MMIO → replay/retry/kill → S3 反压 → clear/refill race与老 store 排序 → SC/AMO → RESET/package 尾部；同时覆盖多个 MSHR completion 和共享资源进度 |
| **4：阶段一验收** | A 与全部 Phase-1 MUST 通过；每个 token 的 bytes/结果正确、effect 至多一次且未完成前不释放；旧 T4 丢写和 T5 混写反例消失；coherence、MMIO、功能 RESET 回归通过；无 owner mismatch、资源泄漏或 watchdog。不能仅凭程序退出、package PASS 或计数相等验收 |

最小波形信号组：

- **Admission：** lane、class、cacheable、primary/secondary/MMIO accept、MSHR id/allocation token、line/set/way、owner/reserved epoch。
- **传播：** S1 lookup owner、S2 raw/effective、MSHR req、RPQ head/inflight、SDQ owner、request/effect token、image dependency。
- **Install：** Grant first/last、all_received、install-owner match、LineBuffer valid/ready/fire、refill first/last fire、provisional/final publish、E fire。
- **Completion：** S3 grant valid、effect fire、terminal tag/status/ACK、RPQ retire、SDQ free、STQ valid/done/retire。
- **进度与 ordering：** freeze/drain/pending、clear/probe/WB fire、older-store block、functional safe、另一 MSHR load response、ROB commit。

验证时以事件身份定位，旧 FST 时间只作参考。沿用 `FST=2*trace_count+half_cycle`，按接受沿前的 valid/ready 判断 fire；新运行另存 source/generated/software manifest。

# 9. Implementation Order

| Step | 文件/module；依赖 | 完成后 invariant |
|---|---|---|
| **1【A】** | MSHRFile 定义 classification、selected owner 和 accept；无前置代码依赖 | `req.fire` 可唯一解释；allocation 不再由旧 conflict 信号推断 |
| **2【A】** | DCache raw/effective payload、destination reservation；依赖 Step 1 | 当前拍地址、owner、token 一致；victim 与 incoming 身份分开 |
| **3【A】** | DCache→MSHR/RPQ/SDQ、secondary、MMIO、replay/S3 传播；依赖 Step 2 | 同一次接收完整捕获；secondary 不重分配，MMIO 不改 slot |
| **4【A】** | DCache/MSHR install permit、mismatch fault；依赖 Step 3 | 合法 refill 推进；错误 owner 不写入、不永久占共享读口 |
| **5【A】** | 全请求构造点、prefetch、共享 write Bundle 审计；依赖已冻结字段契约 | 无遗漏、无效 context 明确；完成 Level 1/2，形成 P0 修复检查点 |
| **6【B】** | LSU/STQ/Hella 功能 token；core package 尾部身份；completion Bundle | 全部功能请求身份独立于统计，复用 slot 不误匹配 |
| **7【B】** | MSHR/RPQ hold、inflight descriptor、tagged terminal、SDQ owner；依赖 Step 6 | retry 不 pop/free；kill、done 和编号复用均有匹配责任 |
| **8【B】** | DCache actual-effect/MMIO terminal→LSU done；老 store issue gate；依赖 Step 7 | accepted 不释放 STQ；年轻 store 不越过未完成老 miss；exactly-once 闭环 |
| **9【B】** | DCache clear/probe/replacement drain、S3 held grant；依赖功能完成闭环 | epoch transition 不越过旧引用；clear/snapshot/install 不覆盖已成功写 |
| **10【B】** | 准确 image generation、provisional/final publish、独立 E；依赖 Step 9 | 每个真实 effect 对应新 image，搬移不造 generation；publish/退休条件完整 |
| **11【B】** | 功能 bitmap/safe/pending 与统计 RESET 分离，更新实施说明和验证状态 | RESET 不改变功能状态；Level 3/4 通过后才声明 Phase-1 closure |

Step 1–5 是一次成组 P0 修复；Step 6–10 的功能接口也必须集成后验证。每步保留清晰 diff 和静态检查点，避免将未闭环的中间接口作为可运行完成版本。

# 3. P0 Resolution

- **P0-1：** MSHR request 从 `s2_effective_req` 完整赋值；epoch、image dependency、request/effect token、transaction/MSHR/SDQ 身份进入同一 acceptance payload，并由 descriptor/RPQ/SDQ companion 捕获。
- **P0-2：** allocation 使用明确 admission class。只有 accepted cacheable primary new-line 创建 incoming owner；secondary 继承现有 owner，MMIO 显式无 cache owner。upgrade 创建 transaction/reservation，但保留 resident epoch/image。
- **RegNext 错拍：** `s2_raw_req = RegNext(s1_req)` 与组合 `s2_effective_req` 分离；当前准入身份不再通过驱动寄存器字段表达。
- **Mismatch：** install/refill mismatch 触发 assertion/fault，禁止 array write 和错误回收；faulty MSHR 停止申请共享 LineBuffer 读口，不转普通 retry。

# 4. Phase-1 Closure

以下 **DONE 表示源码实现完成**，不表示动态验收通过。

| MUST | 状态 | 实现 |
|---|---|---|
| Owner lifecycle | DONE | incoming reservation 与 resident identity 分离；最后 refill data fire 提交新 owner |

| MMIO terminal | DONE | 匹配 D 响应后 held terminal 完成；A acceptance 不释放功能请求 |

| RESET/statistics isolation | DONE | measurement RESET 不清功能 owner/image/token/pending/bitmap/safe |

## 41. 助手 · 2026-10-12T06:40:43.233Z

# Simulation Readiness

## Verdict

NOT_READY_FOR_SIMULATION

## Blocking Issues

- **P0 — AMO.W/AMO.D terminal data is incorrect.**  
  [dcache.scala:1859](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/dcache.scala:1859) stores `s2_data_word(0)` into `s3_result` for every non-SC write, including AMO. This is the raw cache word, while the required architectural AMO return value must use `LoadGen` at [dcache.scala:1767](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/dcache.scala:1767). The held completion then reaches LSU as the AMO result. A narrow AMO can therefore write the wrong value to the destination register.

- **P0 — Refill owner mismatch permanently leaks the MSHR and reservation.**  
  [dcache.scala:1162](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/dcache.scala:1162) forces `refill.ready := false` on mismatch and raises `refill_fault`. [mshrs.scala:206](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/mshrs.scala:206) latches `protocolFault`, but the FSM has no fault-retire, cancel, or relookup transition from `s_commit_line`. The MSHR remains valid, its reservation remains live, and `fence_rdy` cannot recover. This is an explicit permanent-resource-leak path, even though an assertion is raised.

- **P1 — Elaboration and FIRRTL readiness are unverified.**  
  The latest source compile passed, and `git diff --check` plus the strict invariant script passed. However, no target elaboration, initialization/width check, combinational-loop check, generated RTL audit, or simulation has run. The old FST predates this source revision and cannot establish current behavior.

Identity propagation audit:

|---|---|---|---|
| `owner_context_valid` | MSHR admission / MMIO classification | effective request, MSHR, install, refill, S3 | 基本完整；MMIO显式无效 |
| `slot_epoch` | lookup/reservation | MSHR, RPQ, refill, install, S3, completion | 本地路径完整 |
| `line_generation` / `image_valid` | resident image state / replay grant | S3 image check, refill, duplicate guard | 本地路径完整；Phase-2 provenance仍不存在 |
| `transaction_token` | primary MSHR allocation | MSHR, secondary, install, refill, replay result | 完整 |

## Non-blocking Risks

在修复上述 P0 并完成 elaboration/FIRRTL 检查后，首轮应覆盖：

1. 原约 `264000` regression：观察 admission、owner epoch、MSHR token、install/refill fire、owner match、LineBuffer release、ROB commit。

## AMO finding — proven and conditional

File root:
`chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/`

`dcache.scala`:

- 1764: `s2_data_word_prebypass = s2_data_muxed >> (s2_word_idx * coreDataBits)`.
- 1767–1769: existing `LoadGen(mem_size, mem_signed, addr, s2_data_word, s2_sc, wordBytes)`.
- 1776: original load response data is `loadgen(w).data | s2_sc_fail`.
- 1014–1016: writes excluded from `s2_send_resp`, so AMO uses held completion now.
- **1859–1860**:
  ```scala
  s3_result := Mux(duplicate, appliedEffectResult(set)(way),
    Mux(s2_sc, s2_sc_fail.asUInt, s2_data_word(0)))
  ```
- 1968: completion data `s3_result`.
- 1790: terminal response copies completion data.
- LSU `lsu.scala` 1767–1771: AMO register response directly copies `io.dmem.resp.bits.data`.

`rocket/AMOALU.scala` `LoadGen` 33–49 implements 32-bit half selection and sign extension.

Decode 570–571:
`mem_size=inst(13,12)`, `mem_signed=!inst(14)`. AMO.W sign-extends.

Example computed by read-only Python (no simulation):

Raw 64-bit old cache word `0x0000000280000001`:

| address offset | current raw result | correct AMO.W rd |
|---|---|---|
| +0 | `0x0000000280000001` | `0xffffffff80000001` |
| +4 | `0x0000000280000001` | `0x0000000000000002` |

Thus:
- Not every AMO fails.
- Normal AMO.D old 64-bit word already equals full-width architectural old result.
- AMO.W can coincidentally pass if raw entire word equals required selected/sign-extended word; `rd=x0` hides return bug, memory effect may remain correct.
- MMIO AMO goes through `BoomIOMSHR` and uses `loadgen.data` (mshrs 575,622), so this particular bug is cacheable path.
- Original RCA failing transactions are ordinary `M_XWR` stores and loads/MMIO; not AMO.
- Need inspect original program only if necessary to determine later AMO inclusion; don't claim entire benchmark AMO-free from early-window FST.

## Owner propagation and all mutable state locations

### Admission and same-edge capture

`dcache.scala`:

- 807 raw `RegNext(s1_req)` separated from effective Wire.
- 911–962 `s2_effective_req`.
- 923 new-line desired epoch = current `slotEpoch + !tag_match`.
- 927–937 admission sets way/epoch/image/transaction/MSHR/SDQ.
- 938–943 owner context only primary/upgrade/secondary; control paths invalid.
- 944–950 hit uses tag-read epoch.
- 952–960 replay preserves raw owner/token and binds current image dependency.
- **1063** full effective request to MSHR.
- **1071–1082** only `mshrs.req.fire && admission primary/upgrade` captures reservation; primary has new epoch, upgrade same.
- MSHR primary descriptor `req := io.req` at mshrs287; RPQ `enq.bits := io.req` at164; SDQ owner `:= req.bits` at771.
- Secondary mshrs887–897 inherits existing MSHR epoch/way/transaction/image, retains own request/effect token. No destination allocation.
- MMIO mshrs995–997 copy request then owner_context false, way0. No cache reservation.

### All epoch writes

Search `slotEpoch` yields only:

1. DCache **1103–1111**, probe invalidation:
   - epoch++ only `!slotReserved`;
   - if reserved, keep epoch and image validity.
2. DCache **1412–1422**, cross-package clear:
   - recheck pending resident tag/epoch;
   - waits slot conflict/S3/completion/pre-freeze pipeline;
   - epoch++ at clear commit.
3. DCache **2062–2073**, last actual refill row:
   - if current epoch differs reserved epoch, commit reserved epoch.
4. Reset RegInit.

No epoch update from ordinary store, secondary, MMIO, retry, statistics RESET.

### Reservation writes/clear

- Set and identity captured 1071–1082.
- `installActive` false at allocation1077; true install fire1152.
- `slotReserved`/installActive cleared **1193–1198** when matching MSHR valid metadata publish.
- Reset initializes false.

`BoomMSHRFile` permits at most one MSHR per set (`pri_val`853 uses `!idx_match`). Same-set different-tag requests conflict; secondary only same-line. MSHR cannot reuse until state invalid; RPQ/inflight must empty before metadata publish, then E obligation complete before invalid.

### Install/refill mismatch conditions

`dcache.scala`1125–1149:

Install reservation match:
- slotReserved;
- reserved epoch == descriptor;
- transaction == descriptor;
- MSHR ID == descriptor;
- tag == descriptor.
New owner also needs `current epoch +1 == descriptor epoch`.
Upgrade needs owner valid + `current epoch == descriptor`.
Drain separately gates ready; drain waiting does **not** produce fault.

Refill owner match1162–1170:
- owner_context_valid;
- installActive && slotReserved;
- refill epoch/transaction/MSHR/tag match reserved;
- new owner current+1 == reserved; upgrade current == reserved.

1177–1180 faults/assert on true mismatch.

`mshrs.scala`448–482:
- install handshake before any LineBuffer read;
- row hold `rowValid`, captured once; reads only installed && !protocolFault;
- refill fields explicit474–478 from immutable descriptor;
- row counter advances only refill.fire.
- final fire state to drain_rpq; no more refill valid after owner transition.
- no-data Grant install then drain_rpq, no row writes.

`mshrs.scala`206–208:
- protocolFault sticky and assertions.
- under fault lb_read/refill/install valid go false.
- holds MSHR/reservation/RPQ/SDQ, as approved.
- assertion expected stop simulation, so don't portray silent normal deadlock.

### Remaining mismatch-source audit to finish

Need explicitly discuss categories:
1. Lost/defaulted owner fields: known RCA source fixed by full effective capture.
2. Secondary/MMIO duplicate epoch: known RCA source fixed.
3. Premature clear/probe/replacement/reuse: current interlocks; inspect clear/probe paths if needed.
4. Wrong install sequencing/reservation cleared before refill: handshake/state sequencing prevents normal case.
5. Misrouted stale MSHR refill / transaction reuse: IDs/token validation and lifetime.
6. Epoch/token wrap: assertions, not early ordinary regression.
7. External injected invalid identity/protocol violation: fault path; first regression need not recover.

Do not promise absence of all races: no current dynamic validation. Answer “no statically identified remaining source for ordinary original primary+secondary+MMIO path; simulation should test it.”

Potential additional concerns spotted earlier but **not proven / not part of final two findings**:
- Replay S0 gates current S3/Q emptiness without S1 write reservation; potential overlap with independent Hella writes can drop write replay no RETRY, but current PTW only reads and STQ serialized. Avoid introducing broad closure blocker unless concretely relevant.
- Probe allowed before full Grant can invalidate upgrade resident but epoch retained under reservation; coherence behavior dynamic risk.
- Same tag state transition (`Trunk→Dirty`) skips install, but owner reservation still exists for replay, publish clears it; not necessarily bug.
- Last refill predicate uses `cacheDataBeats`, correct current v1 128-bit rows vs 128-bit TL; generalized width risk not first target.

## 48. 助手 · 2026-10-12T07:08:55.214Z

# First Regression Decision

**YES**

理由：可以开始第一轮定向仿真，验证原 264000 路径、secondary、MMIO 和 conflict/replacement。此前将两个问题都归为启动 blocker，范围过大。

**1. AMO terminal result：不是任何 AMO 测试都会错误。**

- **A．静态必然错误：** [s3_result](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/dcache.scala:1859) 保存原始 64-bit word，缺少 AMO.W 的半字选择和符号扩展。只要原始 word 与正确返回值不同，就必然返回错误。例如原 word 为 `0x0000000280000001`，地址低半字的 AMO.W 应返回 `0xffffffff80000001`，当前却返回整个 word。
- **B．异常路径风险：** 这个缺陷无需异常路径即可触发，属于正常 AMO.W 功能错误。
- **C．正常 regression：** AMO.D 的完整 64-bit 返回值不受这个格式缺陷影响；AMO.W 也可能因数据恰好符合要求或 `rd=x0` 而不暴露。当前程序确有 `amoswap.w`，但 UART 锁位于低半字、上半字 padding 清零、锁值为 0/1，这类场景会掩盖缺陷。原 264000 停顿窗口发生在更早的普通 store miss/refill。

**2. refill owner mismatch：未发现修复后原正常路径仍会制造 mismatch 的静态来源。**

核对 [install/refill owner 判定](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/dcache.scala:1125) 后，来源分类如下：

| mismatch 来源 | 当前代码情况 | 正常 regression 判断 |
|---|---|---|
| owner 字段漏接、S2 identity 错拍 | effective payload 同时用于 MSHR capture 和 reservation | 原已证实来源已修复 |
| secondary 重建 owner | 继承主 MSHR 的 way/epoch/transaction，不再次 reservation | 不应产生旧式 mismatch |
| MMIO 污染 cache owner | owner context 无效，不进入 cache reservation | 不应产生旧式 mismatch |
| clear/probe/replacement 提前改变 epoch | clear 等待 owner/drain；probe 对 reserved slot 保留 epoch；replacement 保留旧 epoch 至最终 refill fire | 有静态 interlock，交叠时序需动态验证 |
| install 未激活、reservation 提前释放 | install handshake 后才读 LineBuffer；publish 在 refill/replay 完成后释放 reservation | 未发现正常 FSM 必然违反的路径 |
| 错误 tag/way/MSHR/token、事务提前复用 | descriptor 与 reservation 同拍捕获；refill 使用 descriptor；MSHR 完成后才复用 | 需要身份破坏或遗漏竞态才会触发 |
| epoch/token 耗尽、异常 reset 或故障注入 | 有耗尽 assertion；统计 RESET 不清功能 owner | 属特定异常或边界条件 |

这里 **A 类当前必然错误未成立**。真正的 mismatch 属 **B 类协议故障**：当前 [fault 处理](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/mshrs.scala:206) 会 assertion、禁写，并停止该 MSHR 请求共享 LineBuffer 读口，同时保留请求责任。这符合已选定的 fail-stop 策略，不能仅因未自动 free 就判为第一轮 blocker。

**原 264000 case 覆盖了 C 类正常事务如何因旧实现缺陷产生 mismatch**：MMIO、primary store、同 line secondary、GrantData/refill。它适合验证旧来源是否消失；它没有覆盖当前真实 mismatch 的故障处理，也没有覆盖 clear/probe 交叠。新增 store 串行规则还可能改变原 store-store secondary 时序，因此 secondary 仍需独立定向测试。

# Remaining Static Bugs

## Critical new root-cause lead (must confirm dynamically next)

**Strong likely exact root: physical tag lost by `BoomCacheRefill` inheritance.**

- `phase1.scala`:
  ```scala
  class BoomCacheRefill(implicit p: Parameters)
    extends freechips.rocketchip.rocket.L1DataWriteReq {
      val transaction_token = UInt(32.W)
      val mshr_id = UInt(8.W)
      val owner_context_valid = Bool()
  }
  ```
- Shared `L1DataWriteReq.addr` appears deliberately only **14 bits** (set+block offset), not full physical address.
- Source MSHR `mshrs.scala:470`:
  ```scala
  io.refill.bits.addr := req_block_addr | (refill_ctr << rowOffBits)
  ```
  truncates full address into inherited array address.
- Generated **`BoomMSHR.sv:161`** output:
  ```sv
  output [13:0] io_refill_bits_addr
  ```
- Generated **`BoomMSHR.sv:1304`**:
  ```sv
  assign io_refill_bits_addr = _GEN_27 ? 14'h0 :
      {req_addr[13:6], refill_ctr, 4'h0};
  ```
- DCache source **1162–1170** now checks:
  ```scala
  (mshrs.io.refill.bits.addr >> untagBits) === reservedTag(...)
  ```
  `untagBits` likely14 for nSets256/block64.
  Thus tag from refill array address is **always zero**, but reservedTag for cacheable `0x80087fc0` is nonzero (`0x20021` = `0x80087fc0 >>14`).
- Install uses full `installReq.addr` and succeeds; refill comparator newly added tag check then fails on **first row**, even though epoch/transaction/MSHR/way/context all correct.
- This is **transport metadata identity / owner-check interface bug**, not certificate reset, speculative overwrite, timing change, or permission mismatch.
- Need read `NBDcache.scala` definition and dynamically extract:
  full descriptor addr, refill addr, reserved tag/set/way/epoch/token/MSHR, installActive/slotReserved, individual predicates.
- Keep owner/tag assertion strength; later suggestion add explicit **full physical line/tag sideband** to BoomCacheRefill or explicit owner key separate from narrowed array address, route whole arbitration, preserve array addr semantics. Do NOT remove tag check or widen shared Rocket array addr blindly.

## Design references / scope

Primary spec:
`AI/Design/cache_version_preservation_unified_design_20261011.md` revision2.

Full architecture:
```
ImageKey = {physical_line, content_kind, content_seq, generation={writer_id,image_serial}}
SlotOwnerKey={cache/bank,set,way/buffer,slot_epoch}
safe(K)=known BASE or OWNED seq<=functional_safe
```
- seq0 ≠ BASE proof; UNKNOWN cannot certify.
- actual store/AMO/successful SC/replay write generates new generation; loads/failed SC/retry/validation no generation.
- move/refill preserves accurate key (full cross-layer provenance is Phase2).
- certificate establishes only full bytes commit + matching preservation obligation/ACK.
- invalidated by differing image/write/slot retirement/unknown; late ACK cannot revive cert on different resident.
- validation advances safety only, does not clear identity or cert; parent obligation can retire when successor safe.
- cross-package predecessor P→Q requires safe(P)+preserved(P)+older effects drained; parent copied; Q current cert cleared.
- unsafe Q and safe parent P must preserve recoverability (not permanent double L1 resident).
- L2 same-address predecessor FSM separate from different-address victim FSM; TtoB/BtoT and outer ACK exact completion Phase2.

**Phase1 boundary §§15–17:**
local ownership, full-line install/provisional publish, S2/S3 grant/drain, functional completion/token, RPQ/SDQ/STQ lifetime, single write domain, older-store ordering, local image generation, statistics isolation.
**Phase2**: lower/outer preserved certificates, L2 predecessor, TtoB/BtoT, TL provenance, preserve_done/late ACK.
Do not accuse Phase1 of new certificate maintenance when those fields are not implemented. User asks complete context: summarize desired contracts, show not active in this boot failure.

## 66. 助手 · 2026-10-12T10:49:03.682Z

已完成系统性 RCA，**没有修改功能代码、编译、生成 RTL 或重新仿真**。

# 结论

当前终端错误是：

```text
BoomMSHR.sv:578
assert(~io_refill_fault)
```

对应源码：

```text
mshrs.scala:208
assert(!io.refill_fault, "active refill owner mismatch")
```

实际根因不是 owner epoch、transaction、MSHR ID 或 FSM race，而是：

```text
BoomCacheRefill.addr 只有 14 位数组地址
dcache.scala 却把它当作完整物理地址提取 tag
```

当前首个 refill：

```text
full address       = 0x80087ff0
reserved tag       = 0x20021
refill array addr  = 0x3fc0
refill addr >> 14  = 0
```

因此必然得到：

```text
0 != 0x20021
```

波形 FST2861 显示：

- owner context：匹配
- installActive：匹配
- slotReserved：匹配
- epoch：`1 == 1`
- transaction：`1 == 1`
- MSHR ID：`0 == 0`
- resident epoch 关系：匹配
- physical tag：唯一失败条件

所以这是：

- **a. 物理版本身份传输/解释错误**
- **e. owner 检查产生的假 mismatch**

FSM 卡在 `s_commit_line` 是下游结果。没有证据表明本次首因是 preservation certificate、validation watermark、L2 eviction、TtoB/BtoT、permission 或 timing race。

# 为什么从 264000 变成约 1000

旧日志的 `[264000]` 实际是 watchdog 延迟报错，并不代表前 264000 个 cycle 正常。

旧波形中，同一条 line 在首个 refill 附近已经发生：

```text
primary descriptor epoch 与 slot epoch 不一致
secondary 错误地再次推进 owner
refill 被 backpressure
MSHR 长时间占用 LineBuffer
最后 watchdog 报错
```

当前版本中：

```text
owner propagation 已正确到达 epoch=1
install 在 FST2857 成功
新增 physical tag 检查在 FST2861 立即发现错误
```

因此“提前”主要是错误检测更早，同时当前新增的 tag 接口确实破坏了正常 refill 路径。

# 当前回归判断

当前代码不能开始正常 regression。首个普通 cacheable miss 就可能触发该断言。

本次 request 是普通 `M_XWR`，不是 AMO；AMO.W 的独立 terminal result 问题没有触发本次错误。

# 修复策略

建议修改以下接口，当前未实施：

1. [phase1.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/phase1.scala:53)

   为 `BoomCacheRefill` 增加完整、对齐的 physical line sideband。保留原有 14 位 `addr` 的数组 set/row 语义。

2. [mshrs.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/mshrs.scala:470)

   从 immutable MSHR descriptor 产生完整 line address，并经 refill arbiter 传递。所有 refill beat 必须保持同一 line、epoch、transaction、MSHR ID。

3. [dcache.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/dcache.scala:1162)

   使用完整 physical line sideband 做 tag 校验，同时继续用原 `addr` 校验 set/row。保留所有 owner mismatch 断言，不降低检查强度。

4. 增加逐条件波形和断言，覆盖：

   - full line address
   - reserved tag
   - set/way
   - epoch
   - transaction
   - MSHR ID
   - refill first/last fire
   - 最终 owner publish

## Confirmed RCA from prior turn

- `BoomCacheRefill` inherits Rocket `L1DataWriteReq`.
- `L1DataReadReq.addr = Bits(untagBits.W)`.
- v1Config `untagBits = 14`.
- It contains array set + block row/offset bits, not tag. It is a byte-style array address with low row-offset zeros, **not just a raw numerical SRAM row index**.
- New DCache comparator wrongly calculates `(refill.addr >> untagBits)` as physical tag.
- Generated RTL reduces check to `reservedTag == 0`.

First ordinary cacheable store transaction:

```
MSHR descriptor req.addr = 0x80087ff0
physical line           = 0x80087fc0
refill array addr       = 0x3fc0
reservedTag             = 0x20021
computed erroneous tag  = 0x3fc0 >> 14 = 0
set=255, way=0, epoch=1, transaction=1, MSHR=0
```

All other owner predicates match. First active refill at FST2861 has valid1, ready0, fault1, array arbiter ready1/valid0. Assertion next undumped rising2863:
`BoomMSHR.sv:578`, source `mshrs.scala:208`, `active refill owner mismatch`.

No semantic write/replay/clear/WB before abort. Package0, watermark0. Not AMO or certificate root cause.

Time:
`FST = 2 * trace_count + half_cycle`; final low2862 corresponds harness trace_count1431. `[1000]` is Verilator rounded/formatted time (`VL_TIME_UNITED_Q(1000)`), not exact cycle.
Old saved log is `[264000]`, not26400. Old case already blocked at boot refill ~FST2859, eventually watchdog. Not “correct until264000”.

Prior turn mistakenly sent commentary announcing a patch based on stale user authorization, **immediately corrected it and made no functional edit**. Latest user explicitly wants plan, do not repeat this mistake.

- BOOM and Rocket share this base type. Widening shared addr affects reads, WB array requests, pipeline writes, Rocket cache, array ports.

```
class BoomCacheRefill ... extends L1DataWriteReq {

```

### MSHR producer and routing

## Recommended direction being developed

**A: add `line_addr` only to BoomCacheRefill**, derived combinationally from existing held primary req_block_addr.

- Keep inherited addr width/semantics exactly.
- No new reg/table/metadata authority; existing `req.addr` remains sole source.
- Entire sideband travels in same Bundle/refill arbiter as token/id/row/data.
- Tag check uses line_addr; check line_addr block alignment and set equality with existing addr.
- Retain all epoch/txn/MSHR/context/install/reservation assertions and gates.
- No additional FSM state, pipeline register, LB read, ready-valid handshake, timing cycle, TL field, or cert change.
- Wider payload/mux and a set-equality condition have physical area/combinational timing cost; **do not promise zero synthesis timing cost**.
- Minimal functional edits conceptually 3 files: phase1.scala, mshrs.scala producer, dcache.scala comparator. MSHRFile whole-Bundle plumbing needs audit but likely no new explicit wiring.
- Keep line_addr constant across rows; array addr advances row; both derived from same req.
- Width unresolved final recommendation:
  - `coreMaxAddrBits.W` matches existing descriptor req.addr (current 40bits), preserves exact value without new narrowing and not hardcoded;
  - physical paddrBits currently32 and max virtual extent40. `HasL1HellaCacheParameters` inherits HasCoreParameters, so coreMaxAddrBits accessible to subclass.
  - Could choose paddrBits32 with explicit high-zero assertion, but this adds assumptions. Prefer **same width as descriptor coreMaxAddrBits** for minimal lossless local transport; explain physical content though carrier40.
  - Do not confuse current20bit reservedTag with `paddrBits-untagBits`18; tagBits from pgUntagBits12 dueVM. Retain existing tag computation conventions; don’t redesign tagwidth.

## A/B/C comparison nuances

A:
- BOOM-only specialized Bundle/arbiter grows by descriptor address width.
- Shared array types unchanged; discard sideband at existing projection.
- No latency change, allocation/owner/certificate behavior unchanged.
- Compatible with phase1; best satisfies explicit preserve-addr requirement.

B:
- Cannot widen by assigning bigger value to existing14-bit field.
- If only BoomCacheRefill address is overridden/restructured, its semantic becomes physical, needs explicit narrowing to array port and consumer audit; breaks requested original addr contract.
- If shared L1DataReadReq widened, affects BOOM/Rocket reads/writes/WB arrays and SRAM index expressions (duplicated array waddr uses entire >>rowOffBits). Needs all array addressing explicitly bounded.
- Could be made functionally correct but broad impact, violates user keep array semantics, unnecessary.
- No inherent new pipeline stage, but wider datapath/adapters and regression scope; cannot claim latency change inevitable.

C:
- Can use existing `owner_line_addr(mshr_id)` combinationally without new storage, descriptor remains sole authority.
- Requires current ID in range, owner_line_valid, same allocation txn/epoch/way/set checks; no ready/valid circular path.
- Adds separate address-selection mux/fanout to owner comparator/ready path.
- No necessary new latency, array semantics retained.
- Correct only with strict selection/lifetime proof; using install port current bits is **wrong** because install arbiter can select a different transaction or valid0 after install.
- Consumer coupling stronger, payload not self-contained. A has atomic field arbitration, easier review/prove, prefer A.

## Verification plan must include all requested scenarios

Before mutation list static/layered checks then matrix:

- Scala compile, v1Config elaboration/FIRRTL/comb loops; check addr still14 and line_addr descriptor width; only BOOM specialized ports change; array/TL/Rocket unchanged.
- Normal load/store miss nonzero tags, multiple sets/ways, same set different tag negative.
- First row: complete Grant/LB capture+install ack precede writes, accurate full line/tag; old epoch retained until last actual write, first row backpressure holds source payload.
- Last row: counter increments only fire; last valid without ready does not publish; exact one owner commit; replay+terminal afterward.
- Eviction/refill overlap: same set/way victim different physical tag; WB snapshot uses old victim, refill sideband incoming new line, held C/ACK/data/terminal backpressure; old refs drain; byte scoreboard.
- Version transition: same package repeated writes generation advance only effects; cross-package transition original gate/drain/owner rules remain, line_addr independent seq/gen; reload different tag must not inherit old attribution; validation during pending refill does not rewrite descriptor/sideband/owner.
- Preservation certificate staying: current full certificates not implemented (Phase2). **Do not fabricate certificate signal or claim dynamic certificate proof possible now.**
  - Scope check patch onlylineaddr/add comparator; no writes to cert/key/generation/parent/watermark paths.
  - Current phase1 tests validation/measurement reset mid-refill preserve live descriptor/owner; no new cert established from refill/E.
  - When existing future certificate-capable harness is available, known K with cert and permission-only no-data Grant preserves K/gen/parent/cert; unrelated refill doesn't affect K; distinct image invalidates attached cert per original policy; same-key relocation preserve fact subject onlyafter genuine proof. Mark this full certificate test **pending Phase2**, not a blocker to minimal interface correction.
- Negative tag-only mismatch same set/epoch/txn/id/context → assertion/noarraywrite; wrong txn/epoch/MSHR continue fault; no retries silently retire responsibility.
- MultiMSHR arbitration with blocked writes, check **atomic Bundle from selected input on fire**, not assume output stable arbitrated selection unconditionally.
  - Standard Decoupled Arbiter may switch chosen input when new higher-priority valid appears during stall; each source held row is stable, all fields track same selected source. **Do not impose global aggregate valid&&!ready stable across selection change or add locking arbiter as unsolicited architecture change.**
- no-data upgrade: zero refill rowfires, no invented line identity/cert; existing install/E/replay preserved.
- hit/MMIO/control nonregression, newfield meaningless when valid0 and not persistent.

Waveform common fields:
primary req.addr/block_addr, install req address/id/txn/epoch/fire, rawrefill.valid/ready, newline_addr, inheritedaddr, computedtag/reservedtag/tagmatch/setmatch, set/way/ownerchecks, refillctr/rowValid, dataarbfire, lastfire, slotEpoch/reservedEpoch/reservation/installActive, MSHR allocation token, replay/RPQ/SDQ/STQ terminal. Don't add massivemetadata mirror.

## 70. 助手 · 2026-10-12T11:09:21.446Z

现有代码有一个适合方案 A 的边界：DCache 在完成 owner 校验后，只把 `L1DataWriteReq` 的字段送入数组写口。因此，完整 line 地址可以随 refill 事务传递，并在校验后丢弃，不必改变数组接口。方案 C 也有现成的 MSHR 地址观察端口可用，但需要额外证明它与当拍 refill 仲裁结果属于同一事务。

## 71. 助手 · 2026-10-12T11:12:26.519Z

建议采用方案 A：从 MSHR 已锁存的 `req.addr` 派生 block 对齐的 `line_addr`，随同一个 refill Bundle 传递。原 `addr` 继续用于 set、bank 和 row 寻址；owner 校验改用 `line_addr` 提取 tag，并检查两者的 set 一致。验证计划会分别检查首末 row 的实际写入，以及错 tag 仍被拒绝；完整 certificate 的动态验证则按现有设计的 Phase-2 边界记录。

## 72. 助手 · 2026-10-12T11:13:45.556Z

**推荐方案 A：仅在 `BoomCacheRefill` 增加完整 physical line address sideband。** 原 `addr` 保留数组寻址语义；新字段直接来自已锁存的 MSHR descriptor，并随现有 refill 仲裁传递。修复范围是地址接口与 tag 校验，不改变 Phase-1 FSM、owner 生命周期或版本保存规则。

本轮没有修改代码，也没有编译、生成 RTL 或运行仿真。

**1. 现有接口契约与消费者**

定义入口：[NBDcache.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/rocket-chip/src/main/scala/rocket/NBDcache.scala:20)、[phase1.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/phase1.scala:53)、[mshrs.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/mshrs.scala:470)、[dcache.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/dcache.scala:1162)。

`BoomCacheRefill` 继承 `L1DataWriteReq.addr`，位宽是 `untagBits`，当前配置为 14 位。它包含 **set、line 内 row/offset 的寻址位**，不包含 physical tag。严格说，它是保留低地址位的 array address，不是已经右移后的纯 SRAM row index。

| 位置 | 对 `addr` 的使用 | 判断 |
|---|---|---|
| `mshrs.scala:470` | 完整 block 地址加 row offset，赋入窄字段后保留数组地址位 | 正确的生产端 |
| MSHRFile refill 端口及 `refill_arb` | 仲裁、转发整个 Bundle | 不解释地址 |
| `dcache.scala:1162` | 提取 destination set | 正确 |
| `dcache.scala:1169` | 右移 `untagBits`，当作 physical tag | **唯一已确认的错误解释** |
| `dcache.scala:1174` | 将基础 `L1DataWriteReq` 字段投影到数组写仲裁器 | 正确 |
| `BoomDuplicatedDataArray` | `addr >> rowOffBits` 选择 SRAM row | 正确 |
| `BoomBankedDataArray` | 从地址位选择 bank、bank 内 row | 正确 |
| `dcache.scala:2059–2066` | 判断最后 row、选择 set，并在实际写入后提交 epoch | 正确 |

方案 A 会作用于所有进入 row-install 的 GrantData 路径，包括普通 load/store miss、prefetch，以及返回数据的其他 refill。无数据 upgrade 不产生 refill row；hit、MMIO、WB 数组寻址不需要改变接口。

**2. 三个方案比较**

| 项目 | A：增加 physical line sideband | B：扩大现有 `addr` | C：消费者从 descriptor 恢复地址 |
|---|---|---|---|
| RTL 范围 | BOOM refill payload、生产端和 owner comparator | 若改共享类型，会扩散到 BOOM/Rocket 数组、读写及 WB 接口；若只重构 BOOM 类型，也需要数组转换边界 | DCache 增加按 MSHR ID 选择 descriptor 地址的逻辑 |
| Chisel 接口 | 只扩展 `BoomCacheRefill`；现有整 Bundle 连接随之传递 | 改变原字段类型或语义，并审查所有转换连接 | 可以复用现有 `owner_line_addr/valid/way`，不必新增地址寄存器 |
| Pipeline | 无新增级、寄存器或 FSM 状态 | 不必增加级，但需要更多接口适配 | 无必需新增级，但增加独立 descriptor 选择 mux |
| Refill timing | 保留握手、row counter 和提交时机；payload/mux 变宽，有组合路径成本 | 不必增加周期，但寻址截取和接口范围扩大 | 地址选择进入 owner/ready 判断路径，需要检查组合路径 |
| 已有 cache 路径 | 数组继续收到原窄地址 | 原数组契约改变，验证范围明显扩大 | 数组契约保留，但消费者依赖另一条观察路径的选择与生命周期 |
| Phase-1 适配 | 符合现有 transaction owner 语义 | 可以实现正确，但不满足“保留原 `addr` array 语义”的要求 | 可以正确，但必须证明 descriptor 与当拍 refill 属于同一 allocation |

**C 是可行方案，不应直接判为错误。** 当前确实有 `owner_line_addr`，来自各 MSHR 保存的 request。但需要检查 ID 范围、descriptor 有效性，以及 token、epoch、way、set 与 refill 的一致性。

A 更容易证明：地址、row data、MSHR ID、transaction token、epoch 经过**同一个仲裁选择**，不需要消费者另选一份 descriptor。它新增的是地址传输字段，没有新增独立 metadata 存储或权威来源。

**3. 实施前验证计划**

先做 Scala compile、`v1Config` elaboration、FIRRTL/组合环检查，并确认：

- 原 `addr` 仍为 `untagBits`，当前为 14 位。
- 数组接口与 TileLink 接口没有因 sideband 扩宽。
- tag 比较使用完整 line 地址，不再被化简成 `reservedTag == 0`。

随后进行以下定向验证：

| 场景 | 必须观察的行为 |
|---|---|
| 普通 cache miss | load/store、非零 tag、不同 set/way 正常 refill；最终读回字节正确。原 line `0x80087fc0` 的比较 tag 应为 `0x20021` |
| First refill row | 完整 GrantData 捕获及 install 握手按原流程完成；首 row 地址、data、owner 同源；stall 时该 MSHR 持有的 row 不变 |
| Last refill row | counter 只随 `fire` 推进；最后 row 仅 valid、未 ready 时不提交 owner；实际最后写入后按原规则提交 epoch |
| Eviction/refill 交叉 | 同 set/way 的旧 victim 与 incoming line 使用各自地址；WB snapshot 保持旧 image，refill 写入新 image；加入写仲裁和 Release/ACK 延迟 |
| Version transition | 同包语义写按原规则推进 generation；跨包沿用原 gate/drain；新字段不改变 seq、generation、watermark 或 owner 更新条件 |
| Preservation 保持 | 检查本次改动没有额外修改版本、parent、证书相关状态；validation/measurement RESET 不改变 pending refill descriptor 和 owner |
| 多 MSHR 仲裁 | 每次输出的地址、data、token、epoch、ID 都来自同一个选中输入，特别覆盖写口阻塞期间仲裁候选变化 |
| 负向 owner 检查 | 同 set、正确 token/epoch，但错误 physical tag 时仍触发 fault/assert；禁止数组写入和责任释放。错误 token/epoch/MSHR 同样拒绝 |
| 无数据 upgrade | refill row 数为零；原 install、E、replay 流程保持有效 |

关于 certificate：[现有设计边界](/data1/gzh/EC/AI/Design/cache_version_preservation_unified_design_20261011.md:427) 将完整证书链放在 Phase-2，当前 working tree 尚未实现这些证书。因此，本次可以验证其**维护逻辑未被地址修复改变**，不能宣称完整 certificate 动态验证通过。证书实现接通后，应补测同 key 合法搬移保持事实、纯权限升级保持身份、不同 image 按原规则处理证书。

通过定向验证后再运行原 regression，验收实际数据结果与事务完成，不能只看 assertion 消失或 package PASS。

**4. 最小修改计划**

| 文件、位置 | 修改内容 |
|---|---|
| `phase1.scala`，`BoomCacheRefill` | 新增 `line_addr`，定义为 block 对齐的完整 physical line 地址；位宽沿用 descriptor 地址类型 `coreMaxAddrBits`，避免新截断及硬编码 |
| `mshrs.scala`，`s_commit_line` refill payload 构造处 | 从现有 `req_block_addr` 驱动 `line_addr`；原 `addr`、row data、counter、owner 字段和握手逻辑保留 |
| `dcache.scala`，`refillReqOwnerMatch` | 使用 `line_addr >> untagBits` 比较 `reservedTag`；同时检查 `line_addr` 的 set 与数组 `addr` 的 set 一致 |
| 同一 DCache 校验边界 | 增加 valid 时的 line 对齐检查；保留全部 context、reservation、token、MSHR、epoch 检查及原 fault/assert |
| MSHRFile 连接与数组字段投影 | 审查整 Bundle 连接完整传递新字段；利用现有基础字段投影，在数组写口前丢弃 sideband |

不能从 `reservedTag` 拼出新地址，否则校验值来自被校验对象，会失去检测能力。也不能借用当拍 `install.bits.req`：install 握手之后该端口可能无效，或已仲裁到另一事务。

**新增 signal 的生命周期**

- Primary admission 已将完整 request 地址锁存在 `req`。
- `line_addr` 从这份 descriptor 组合派生，不增加地址寄存器。
- 一次 refill 的各 row 使用相同 `line_addr`；原 `addr` 随 row counter 变化。
- 两者随同一个 refill Bundle 仲裁，并在数组写入前完成校验。
- 最后 row 完成后不新增 resident 地址副本；MSHR 按原生命周期退出。

这不会把 physical line 地址当作完整 ImageKey，也不改变现有 generation、imported-image 或 certificate 语义。

**5. 新增 waveform 检查**

建议保留以下窄信号组：

- 地址来源：MSHR `req.addr`、`req_block_addr`、新增 `line_addr`、原 array `addr`、row counter。
- 校验：提取的 tag、`reservedTag`、set 一致性、现有 owner predicates、`refillReqOwnerMatch`。
- 握手：**原始** `mshrs.refill.valid/ready`、数组输入 valid/fire、first/last row、install fire、LineBuffer read fire。
- 生命周期：transaction token、MSHR ID、reserved/resident epoch、`installActive`、RPQ issue/inflight/terminal。

现有 debug refill valid 是经过 owner 门控后的数组输入 valid；必须同时观察原始 refill valid，才能区分“生产端没有请求”和“校验拒绝请求”。

方案 A 的最小正确性依据是：**补齐缺失的物理身份传输，让原 owner 检查比较正确对象，同时保留数组地址契约和全部错误检测能力。**

## Progress and likely confirmed root cause

**New failure is direct assertion, not watchdog.**

Current log:
```
[625000] %Error: BoomMSHR.sv:579:
TOP.TestHarness.chiptop.system.tile_prci_domain.tile_reset_domain_boom_tile.dcache.mshrs.mshrs_3.assert__assert_4
```

Generated RTL `BoomMSHR.sv:579`:
```
assert__assert_4: assert(~io_refill_fault);
```
Source `mshrs.scala:208`:
```
assert(!io.refill_fault, "active refill owner mismatch")
```

**Root cause evidence now very strong / effectively confirmed:**
`rowValid` / `rowData` capture uses **shared** `io.lb_read.fire` without qualification to install-row reads. Although the Scala code is textually inside `s_commit_line`, generated RTL updates these registers for LineBuffer reads in **`s_drain_rpq_loads` too**. A normal load bypass reads row 2 before install ownership is activated. This sets the install row holding register valid. When FSM later enters `s_commit_line`, refill valid is immediately asserted using this unrelated row capture, with installStarted=0 / installActive=0. Owner guard correctly rejects it and asserts.

Do not present this as another physical-tag problem or merely suppress `refill.valid`: underlying row capture ownership must be corrected. Potential wrong-row write if only adding delay/valid gate without clearing/reacquiring correctly.

Relevant current source:
`chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/mshrs.scala`

- `s_drain_rpq_loads` lines360–405:
  - `io.lb_read.valid := rpq.io.deq.valid && drain_load`
  - offset from RPQ request address
  - load response bypasses installation
- `s_commit_line` lines448–484:
  ```
  val rowValid = RegInit(false.B)
  val rowData = Reg(UInt(encRowBits.W))
  io.lb_read.valid := grant_had_data && installStarted && !protocolFault && !rowValid
  ...
  when (io.lb_read.fire) { rowData := io.lb_resp; rowValid := true.B }
  io.refill.valid := grant_had_data && rowValid && !protocolFault
  ```
  Refill valid lacks installStarted but originally relies on provenance of rowValid. Root defect is unqualified capture.
  rowValid clears only reset / refill.fire, not primary admission.

**Generated RTL explicitly proves the lost state guard:**
Saved `BoomMSHR.sv`:
- line442:
  ```
  _T_296 = io_lb_read_ready & _io_lb_read_valid_output
  ```
- line486:
  ```
  _io_lb_read_valid_output =
    ... (state==s_drain_rpq_loads ? rpq_valid & drain_load
         : state==s_commit_line & grant_had_data & installStarted & !fault & !rowValid)
  ```
- line501:
  ```
  _io_refill_valid_output = ... state==s_commit_line & grant_had_data & rowValid & !protocolFault
  ```
- line860:
  ```
  rowValid <= ~_T_297 & (_T_296 | rowValid)
  ```
- lines965–966:
  ```
  if (_T_296) rowData <= io_lb_resp
  ```
No `state==s_commit_line` guard on `_T_296` captures. Lexical placement of registers inside Chisel when does not establish register lifetime guard; generated control illustrates this exact issue.

## 99. 助手 · 2026-10-12T13:54:01.652Z

### Failure Root Cause

这是直接断言，不是 watchdog：

- `mshrs_3.assert__assert_4`
- `mshrs.scala:208`: `assert(!io.refill_fault)`
- 失败信号：`dcache.mshrs.io_refill_fault_3`
- 首个 fault 信号：FST `1250563`，约 cycle `625281`
- 断言下一次采样约在 cycle `625282`
- MSHR3：`s_commit_line`，transaction `937`

真正的 first divergence 在 **cycle 625269**：

1. transaction 937 请求普通 `M_XRD`
2. GrantData 已完整进入 LineBuffer
3. MSHR 仍在 `s_drain_rpq_loads`，`installStarted=0`
4. 普通 load 的 `io.lb_read.fire` 错误地更新了安装用 `rowValid`
5. 后续进入 `s_commit_line` 时，`rowValid=1`，但 `installActive=0`
6. `refill.valid` 被发出，DCache owner 检查正确拒绝，触发 `refill_fault`

相关代码位于 [mshrs.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/mshrs.scala:360) 和 [mshrs.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/mshrs.scala:448)。

### Owner 检查结果

本次 transaction 中以下字段均匹配：

- physical line：`0x80007d80`
- array/set：set `246`
- physical tag：`0x20001`
- MSHR：`3`
- transaction：`937`
- epoch：`13`
- reservation tag、MSHR、transaction、epoch
- `installReservationMatch=1`
- `installOwnerMatch=1`

唯一失败项是：

```text
installActive = 0
```

因此这不是 Scheme A 的 `line_addr` 错误。`line_addr` 已正确参与 tag validation。

### 关键时间线

| Cycle | 事件 |
|---:|---|
| 625253 | 捕获 transaction 937，地址 `0x80007da8`，line `0x80007d80` |
| 625260-625267 | 四个 GrantData beat 写入 LineBuffer |
| 625268 | `s_drain_rpq_loads` 服务普通 load，LineBuffer offset 2 读取成功 |
| **625269** | `rowValid` 在 `installStarted=0` 时被置 1，首次违反安装行 provenance |
| 625270-625280 | metadata read/clear 与 victim writeback |
| 625281 | `s_commit_line` 发出 `refill.valid`，但 `installActive=0` |
| 约 625282 | `assert(!io_refill_fault)` 触发 |

全程扫描得到 22,449 次 `rowValid` 捕获，其中只有这 1 次发生在 install 状态之外。

### 分类排序

1. **A. refill/install 生命周期问题：约 99%，已确认**  
   安装行寄存器错误接收了普通 load 数据。

2. **E. timing/provenance 控制问题：约 1%**  
   本质是共享 ready/fire 控制缺少读取目的限定，不是独立异步 race。

3. **B. version identity：当前不是首因**  
   `req_line_generation=40971` 是可见的依赖快照，完整 ImageKey/certificate 未在此断言前提交。

4. **C. L2/outer：无首因证据**  
   GrantData、Grant E 和 victim writeback response 均已完成。

5. **D. validation/watermark：无首因证据**  
   未观察到 validation 推进先于该 divergence；watermark 保持为 0。

### 为什么现在能运行到 625000

Scheme A 已修复 physical tag 来源，因此本次运行成功完成：

- 22,448 次 refill row write
- 5,612 次完整四行安装
- 最后 5,000-cycle 窗口内完成 36 条完整 line install

此前约 1000 cycle 的 physical-tag fault 已消失。当前长期失败暴露的是另一个安装行 provenance bug。

最小修复方向是让 `rowValid/rowData` 只接受明确的 install-row read，并绑定 `s_commit_line`、`installStarted` 和当前 owner；同时保留 `installActive` 的 refill 防护。不能通过屏蔽 `refill_fault` 或延迟断言解决。

完整报告：[phase1_625000_row_capture_rca_20261012.md](/data1/gzh/EC/AI/Debug/history/reports/phase1_625000_row_capture_rca_20261012.md:1)。本轮未修改功能代码、未重新生成 RTL、未运行新仿真。

## 103. 助手 · 2026-10-12T14:08:51.067Z

### 1. `rowValid`语义

当前设计中：

- `grant_had_data`：表示完整 GrantData 已进入 LineBuffer。
- `rowValid`：应表示某个安装 row 已从 LineBuffer 读入 `rowData`，可以提交 refill。
- 普通 load 的 LineBuffer response 不应修改 `rowValid`。

因此设计意图是 **B**，但当前生成 RTL 实际表现为 **C**。共享的 `io.lb_read.fire` 同时覆盖：

- `s_drain_rpq_loads` 的普通 load read
- `s_commit_line` 的 install row read

所以普通 load 会污染安装用 `rowValid/rowData`。

### 2. `rowValid` 所有写入口

[src/mshrs.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/mshrs.scala:463)

| 操作 | 位置 | 当前条件 | 结论 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 初始化为 0 | `RegInit(false.B)` | reset | 正确 |
| 写入 1 | line 468 | `io.lb_read.fire` | 错误，未限定 install provenance |
| 清为 0 | line 482 | `io.refill.fire` | 正确 |

没有设计上的 RPQ/replay/forwarding 写入口。问题是普通 load 的 `io.lb_read.fire` 被错误地复用了。

`installStarted` 只在 `io.install.fire` 后置位。由于：

```text
io.install.fire = io.install.valid && io.install.ready
io.install.ready = installOwnerMatch && installDrain
```

所以 `installStarted` 已经是 owner 被 DCache 接受的注册化证明。无需新增 raw `installOwnerMatch` 跨模块组合信号。

### 3. 三种方案

| 方案 | 影响 | 评价 |
|---|---|---|
| A：限制 `rowValid` 写入 | 只增加显式 install-read 条件；不改 Bundle、FSM、TileLink | 最小，推荐 |
| B：拆分 `lineBufferDataValid` / `installRowValid` | 增加状态、清除路径和验证面；`grant_had_data` 已承担大部分前者语义 | 正确但过度设计 |
| C：增加 `rowOwner` | 增加 owner metadata 和比较逻辑 | owner 不足以区分普通 load，因为普通 load 与 install 属于同一个 MSHR/transaction；仍需要 purpose bit |

### 4. 推荐方案 A

建议将 `rowValid/rowData` 提升到 MSHR 稳定寄存器作用域，并定义明确的捕获条件：

```text
install_row_read_fire =
    io.lb_read.fire &&
    state === s_commit_line &&
    grant_had_data &&
    installStarted &&
    !protocolFault
```

只有 `install_row_read_fire` 才能执行：

```text
rowData  := io.lb_resp
rowValid := true
```

同时：

- 新 primary request 在 `handle_pri_req` 时清除 `rowValid`
- `io.refill.fire` 时继续清除 `rowValid`
- 普通 load drain 只产生 `io.resp`，不写 `rowData/rowValid`
- 保留 DCache `installActive`、MSHR、transaction、epoch、tag、line_addr 和所有 fault/assert 检查

不建议新增 DCache 到 MSHR 的 raw `installOwnerMatch` 组合反馈；`io.install.fire` 已经包含 owner match 和 drain 条件。

### 5. FSM和时序影响

FSM 状态转移不变：

```text
s_commit_line
  -> io.install.fire
  -> installStarted=1 / installActive=1
  -> 下一周期允许 install LineBuffer read
  -> rowValid=1
  -> refill.fire
```

因此：

- 普通 load drain 行为不变
- refill 协议不变
- 不增加正常路径周期
- `rowValid/rowData` 在 refill backpressure 时保持稳定
- owner 失效时仍由 DCache `installActive` 检查拒绝 refill

### 6. 验证计划

必须确认：

1. `s_drain_rpq_loads` 的 `io.lb_read.fire` 不改变 `rowValid/rowData`。
2. `io.install.fire` 之前不能出现 `rowValid=1`。
3. `rowValid=1` 必须满足 `installStarted=1` 和 `grant_had_data=1`。
4. `refill.valid` 仍经过未修改的 `installActive` owner check。
5. 多 MSHR 交叉读取不能改变其他 MSHR 的 row state。
6. refill `ready=0` 时 `rowValid/rowData` 保持不变。
7. transaction 937 不再出现 `installActive=0 && refill.valid=1`。
8. `refill_fault` 和 `assert(!io.refill_fault)` 仍然存在且未被弱化。

完整设计记录已保存于 [cache_phase1_row_provenance_fix_20261012.md](/data1/gzh/EC/AI/Design/cache_phase1_row_provenance_fix_20261012.md:1)。本轮未修改功能代码。

[mshrs.scala](/data1/gzh/EC/chipyard/generators/boom/src/main/scala/lsu/mshrs.scala)

修改内容：

- 新 primary request 时清除 `rowValid`。
- 删除普通 `io.lb_read.fire` 对 row state 的直接更新。
- 增加 `install_row_read_fire` 条件，仅 install read 可写入：
  - `rowData`
  - `rowValid`

相对于方案 A 之前的快照，没有其他本轮功能修改。当前 working tree 中其他 Phase-1 文件修改属于既有变更。

### B. `rowValid` 生命周期

- reset：`rowValid := false`
- GrantData：仅写入 LineBuffer，不更新 `rowValid`
- 普通 load drain：继续读取 `LineBuffer` 并返回响应，不修改 `rowData/rowValid`
- install read：仅当以下条件同时满足时置位：

```scala
io.lb_read.fire &&
state === s_commit_line &&
installStarted &&
grant_had_data &&
!protocolFault
```

- refill fire：`rowValid := false`
- 新 primary admission：`rowValid := false`

`rowData` 在 refill 后可以保留，但只有 `rowValid` 为真时才会被送入 refill。

### C. Provenance 证明

`rowValid` 唯一置位路径是 `install_row_read_fire`，该条件包含 `installStarted`。

`installStarted` 只有在 install handshake 后置位，并在新 primary admission 时清零；新 primary 同时清零 `rowValid`。因此不会出现：

```text
installStarted = 0 && rowValid = 1
```

生成 RTL 也确认：

- `rowData` 只有 install-row read fire 写入。
- 普通 `s_drain_rpq_loads` 路径没有 row state 写入。
- `refill_fault` 和 owner assert 仍然存在。

### D. 行为影响

- 普通 load 响应路径未改变，仍由 `io.lb_read.fire` 驱动。
- refill backpressure 未改变，`rowValid` 保持到 `io.refill.fire`。
- 每个 MSHR 拥有独立的 row state，不影响多 MSHR 并行。
- 未修改 FSM、refill protocol、owner check、version/generation 或 preservation 逻辑。

- `CheckCombLoops`：通过。

## 当前进展

## 已确认的 guest 链路

源码：

- `Software/Test/test.c`
- `Software/Test/stat/report.c`
- `Software/Test/test.dump`
- `Software/Test/test.riscv`

调用顺序：

```text
gapbs_bfs verified=1
  -> test_report()
  -> printf("[RUN] benchmark=...")
  -> report_end()
     -> 等待 checker ready
     -> wait_for_package_statistics_to_drain()
     -> ghe_fpga_perf_stop()
     -> wait_for_boom_strict_pending()
     -> ...
     -> printf("[PERF] ...")
  -> printf("[END] ...")
  -> main return
  -> _exit()
```

关键事实：

1. `report_end()` 的第一个输出是 `[PERF]`，不是 `_exit`。
2. `test.log` 有 `[RUN] benchmark=... verified=1`，但没有 `[PERF]`、`[END]`。
3. `test.out` 中，进入 `report_end` 后先执行到：

```text
0x8000193c ... 计算格式化参数
0x8000194c jal 0x800048e6 <iprintf>
0x80005b52 _write_r
0x80006600 _write
0x80006636 htif_syscall
0x80006664 sd ... tohost
0x80006666 ld ... fromhost
0x80006668 beqz ... 继续等待
```

4. 因此当前不是“benchmark 后直接进入 `_exit`”，而是卡在第一次 `[PERF]` 输出的 host write syscall。
5. `test.dump` 中：

```text
tohost  = 0x80007c80
fromhost = 0x80007c88
```

`htif_syscall` 约在 `0x80006636`：

```c
buf[0] = n;
buf[1] = a0;
buf[2] = a1;
buf[3] = a2;
sc = HTIF_TOHOST(0, 0, (uintptr_t)&buf);
tohost = sc;
while (fromhost == 0);
```

## 已确认的 syscall 类型和值

反汇编显示：

```text
0x80006600:
    li a3, 64
    j  htif_syscall
```

因此第一笔失败请求是：

```text
syscall number = 64
sys_write
fd             = 1
```

`report_end` 中对应的是：

```c
printf("[PERF] boom_cycles=%lu boom_inst=%lu\n", ...);
```

FST/commit trace 中，失败请求的 syscall buffer 地址为：

```text
&buf = 0x80087bc0
```

所以 guest 写入 `tohost` 的值是：

```text
HTIF_TOHOST(0, 0, 0x80087bc0)
= 0x0000000080087bc0
```

`tohost` store 本身已经在 BOOM commit trace 中出现：

```text
0x80006664 sd a2,0(a3)
```

随后立即循环读取 `fromhost`，读到的值一直是零。

这证明：

- guest 已成功执行并提交 `tohost` store；
- 请求不是被 speculative/squash 遗弃；
- 当前需要判断 host 是否观察到该 store，以及 host 侧是否完成 `sys_write` 和 `fromhost` 回写。

## 已确认的 Chipyard/FESVR/TSI 实现链

当前 `v1Config` 使用：

```text
AbstractConfig
  -> WithSimSerial
  -> serialized TileLink backing memory
```

实际实现：

```text
guest BOOM store to tohost
  -> serialized TileLink / TSI
  -> testchip_tsi_t
  -> tsi_t::host_thread()
  -> htif_t::run()
  -> mem.read_uint64(tohost_addr)
  -> device_list.handle_command()
  -> syscall_t::handle_syscall()
  -> syscall_t::sys_write()
  -> fromhost_queue
  -> mem.write_uint64(fromhost_addr, response)
```

相关源码：

- `chipyard/generators/testchipip/src/main/resources/testchipip/csrc/SimSerial.cc`
- `chipyard/generators/testchipip/src/main/resources/testchipip/csrc/testchip_tsi.cc`
- `chipyard/generators/testchipip/src/main/resources/testchipip/csrc/testchip_tsi.h`
- `chipyard/toolchains/riscv-tools/riscv-isa-sim/fesvr/tsi.cc`
- `chipyard/toolchains/riscv-tools/riscv-isa-sim/fesvr/tsi.h`
- `chipyard/toolchains/riscv-tools/riscv-isa-sim/fesvr/htif.cc`
- `chipyard/toolchains/riscv-tools/riscv-isa-sim/fesvr/syscall.cc`
- `chipyard/toolchains/libgloss/misc/htif.c`
- `chipyard/toolchains/libgloss/include/htif.h`

`htif_t::run()` 的核心逻辑在 `fesvr/htif.cc`：

```cpp
tohost = mem.read_uint64(tohost_addr);

if (tohost != 0)
    mem.write_uint64(tohost_addr, 0);

device_list.handle_command(cmd);

if (!fromhost_queue.empty() && !mem.read_uint64(fromhost_addr))
    mem.write_uint64(fromhost_addr, response);
```

`syscall_t` 中确认：

```cpp
table[64] = &syscall_t::sys_write;
register_command(0, ... handle_syscall ...);
```

因此 host 侧并非没有标准的 `sys_write` handler。

## 最可能的根因方向

尚未最终定案，但当前最强方向是：

```text
BOOM guest 已提交 tohost store
  -> FESVR/TSI 需要通过 serialized TileLink 读取 tohost
  -> 该地址位于正常 DRAM/cache 地址空间
  -> 需要确认这个 store 是否已经对 TSI/DRAM 可见
```

重点候选：

1. `tohost` store 只停留在 BOOM/L1/L2 cache 或 store buffer，TSI host-side memory read看到旧零值。
2. Phase-1 cache/owner/metadata 修改影响了 `tohost` 所在 cache line 的写回或可见性。
3. TSI host thread 收到了请求，但 serialized TileLink 返回/反向响应被阻塞。
4. host `sys_write` 本身阻塞或异常，导致 callback 没有将 response 放入 `fromhost_queue`。概率目前低于前两项，因为 stdout/test.log 管道仍存在。
5. `tohost` 已被读取，但 `fromhost` 回写没有成功完成。需要波形证据区分。

## 下一步必须完成

1. 从 FST 中只筛选精确地址 `0x80007c80`：

```text
40-bit dcache addr binary:
0000000010000000000000000111110010000000
```

筛选：

- `dcache.io__lsu_req_bits_0_bits_addr`
- `dcache.io__lsu_req_bits_0_bits_uop_mem_cmd`
- `dcache.io__lsu_req_bits_0_bits_data`
- `dcache.io__lsu_req_valid`
- `dcache.auto_out_a_valid`
- `dcache.auto_out_a_bits_address`
- L2 A-channel address/valid
- serial TL in/out bits

目标是建立：

```text
tohost guest store
  -> L1/L2 request
  -> serialized TL request
  -> host TSI read of tohost
  -> response
```

2. 对比一个已成功的早期 HTIF `sys_write` 和失败的 `[PERF]` syscall：

- 早期成功 request 的 `tohost` store；
- 失败 request 的 `tohost` store；
- 两者是否都出现 cache/TL writeback；
- 失败请求之后是否出现 host-side serial read of `0x80007c80`；
- 是否出现 `fromhost` 地址 `0x80007c88` 的写回。

3. 检查 `0x80007c80` 是否被系统标记为 uncached/MMIO。当前软件 ELF 将其放在 `.htif`、普通 DRAM 地址空间；默认 Rocket/BOOM cache 可能将其视作普通 cacheable memory。不要假设它自动是 uncached，必须从实际 config/cache实现确认。

4. 检查 FESVR `syscall_t::sys_write()` 的 fd 映射和 host stdout 是否可能阻塞：

```text
chipyard/toolchains/riscv-tools/riscv-isa-sim/fesvr/syscall.cc
```

但不要把“可能”写成结论。重点确认 syscall handler 是否会在 `write(fd=1)` 后调用 response callback。

5. 明确分层结论：

- 已证明：guest `tohost` store committed；
- 未证明：FESVR 是否读到非零 `tohost`；
- 未证明：FESVR 是否执行了 `sys_write`；
- 未证明：`fromhost` response 是否尝试写回；
- 已证明：guest 一直看到 `fromhost == 0`。

## 已收敛的真正根因

**不是 FESVR 漏回写，而是 BOOM 的 cache/MSHR 活锁使已提交的 `tohost` store 一直无法产生写效果，FESVR 始终读到零，因此根本没有 dispatch 这笔 syscall。**

具体链：

1. `tohost=0x80007c80`、`fromhost=0x80007c88` **在同一个 64B cache line**，line base `0x80007c80`、L1 set `242`。
2. BOOM 的年轻 `fromhost` polling load 比已提交但仍在 STQ 的 `tohost` store 先分配 **MSHR 2，transaction `0x4da` (1242)**。
3. primary 是 `M_XRD=0`，锁存 `req.addr=0x80007c88`。GrantData 完整捕获；最后 `new_coh=1` (**Branch/read permission**)。
4. `tohost` 是 `M_XWR=1`。`onSecondaryAccess()` 明确：
   ```scala
   needs_second_acq = isWriteIntent(second_cmd) && !isWriteIntent(first_cmd)
   ```
   因而 store 不能合入读 primary，`io_req_sec_val=1` 但 `io_req_sec_rdy=0`，S2 `nack_miss=1`，STQ 重试。
5. 四个 refill row 都成功写入；owner/tag check 全通过。MSHR 进入 `s_drain_rpq=12 (0xc)`。
6. `sec_rdy` 在 `s_drain_rpq` **仍允许新增 secondary loads**。
7. Phase-1 replay 改为保持 RPQ head，等待 `replay_result` terminal 后出队，吞吐慢于持续 polling loads；RPQ 永远不空。
8. publish 条件：
   ```scala
   when (rpq.io.empty && !rpq.io.enq.valid && !replay_inflight) {
     state := s_meta_write_req
   }
   ```
   永远不满足。MSHR 2 一直停在 12；`io_meta_write_valid=0`，slot reservation/installActive 保持。
9. SRAM 已装完，但 metadata 未最终 publish，store 无法获得写权限或重新分配事务，一直 nack。
10. FESVR/TSI 仍持续读 `tohost`，实际返回 `0`；没有调用 `sys_write`，没有 `cmd.respond(1)`，guest 永远读取 `fromhost==0`。

这是 **cache transaction 的进度/secondary admission/replay drain 活锁**，不是 certificate/watermark 等待。波形中 `crossPackageDomainWait=0`、`l1CrossPackageBlocked=0`、`protocolFault=0`；safe watermark 已 336。

建议方向（尚未最终写报告）：保证 install 后 drain 是有限集合，冻结/限制新 secondary admission，使既有 RPQ+inflight 有界排空、publish，然后让拒绝的 store 重试成为新 upgrade。不要特判 HTIF 地址、不加 guest fence 当功能修复、不弱化 owner/assert、不提前释放 reservation。`tohost/fromhost` 分开 cacheline 可作为诊断/软件布局改进，但不根治 generic MSHR livelock。

来自 FST 10387005/017/029/041 四次 store。

`HTIF_TOHOST(dev=0, cmd=0, payload=&buf)`：
`tohost value = 0x0000000080087bc0`。

ELF `.htif` flags WA，普通 DRAM/cacheable：

```text
tohost  0x80007c80
fromhost 0x80007c88
htif_syscall 0x80006636
_write 0x80006600
_write_r 0x80005b52
report_end 0x8000151c
_exit 0x80006896
```

反汇编：

```text
8000194c jal iprintf    (report_end first PERF printf)
80006600 li a3,64
80006604 j htif_syscall
80006658 fence w,w
80006664 sd a2,0(a3)       tohost
80006666 ld a5,8(a3)       fromhost
80006668 beqz a5,80006666
8000666a auipc...
8000666e sd zero,...       clear fromhost
80006672 amoswap.w.rl ...  unlock
80006676 fence r,r
8000667a ld a0,0(sp)       syscall return result
8000667e ret
```

正常 response `fromhost=1`（device0/cmd0 ACK），实际返回写入 `buf[0]`，正常应44；随后 `_write`/printf 返回至 `report_end` PC `0x80001950` 继续。

`_exit(0)` 发送 `tohost=1`，未到达。
